Unlinked requirements: NFR-04
Collection failures: none

## Manual verification and remaining gaps

NFR-02 requires replacing each agent without editing peers or the orchestrator. The six role-swap cases exercise contract dispatch, persistence and settlement through existing injection seams. Review the Architect parse_input/generate_plan, Generator generate(plan), SecurityStage.run and ValidationStage.run contracts against docs/spec/04-design.tex. Check that replacement implementations retain the documented error/output contracts and require no peer edits. Production recovery uses concrete-class checks, generator.last_run and private _last_run mutation; the contract-double tests do not establish unrestricted replaceability of those recovery/telemetry paths. This coupling remains an explicit architectural limitation; no broad refactor is included in Phase 9b.

NFR-04 remains unlinked/unverified: manually provision a clean Ubuntu 24 environment using author-installed prerequisites, run the full stack and a stub pipeline, and exercise the UI in supported browsers without plugins. This session performs no installs/downloads. The spec's tfsec prerequisite is superseded by accepted D-31 Trivy usage; record actual versions and outcomes. Local tests are not a clean-deployment proof.

PR-01 uses API settlement as the documented D-43 proxy for UI availability; PR-02 measures UI emission-to-DOM separately. PR-03 and PR-04 failures remain failures. An annotation, stub timing or isolated PR-05 pass is not a real-provider or concurrent-workload compliance claim.
